    21	    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
    22	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
    23	            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
    24	        }
    25	    }
    26	}
    27	
    28	void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t rows, const size_t N,
    29	                    const size_t firstRow) {
    30	    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
    31	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
    32	            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, firstRow + i, j);
    33	        }
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	// Multiply local A rows by the complete B matrix.  The k order is identical
    38	// to the original i-j-k kernel, so the floating-point result is unchanged.
    39	// With k as the middle loop, B and each C row are accessed contiguously and
    40	// the innermost loop can be vectorized by the compiler.
    41	void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
    42	                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
    43	    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
    44	        const double* const aRow = A.data() + i * N;
    45	        double* const cRow = C.data() + i * N;
    46	        std::fill(cRow, cRow + N, 0.0);
    47	
    48	        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    49	            const double aValue = aRow[k];
    50	            const double* const bRow = B.data() + k * N;
    51	            for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
    52	                cRow[j] += aValue * bRow[j];
    53	            }
    54	        }
    55	    }
    56	}
    57	
    58	// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
    59	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
    60	                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    61	    // Check a few random positions
    62	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    63	
    64	    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
    65	        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
    66	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
    67	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
    68	
    69	            double expected = 0.0;
    70	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    71	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
    72	            }
    73	
    74	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
    75	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
    76	
    77	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
    78	                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
    79	                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
    80	                return false;
    81	            }
    82	        }
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    return true;
    86	}
    87	
    88	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    89	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    90	    printf("Options:\n");
    91	    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    92	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    93	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    94	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
    95	}
    96	
    97	// MPI point-to-point calls use an int element count on all commonly deployed
    98	// MPI versions.  Chunking keeps the code correct for matrices whose flat
    99	// storage exceeds that limit, while normal benchmark-sized matrices use one
   100	// collective call.
   101	constexpr size_t kMpiChunkElements = 1U << 20;
   102	
   103	void broadcastMatrix(std::vector<double>& matrix, MPI_Comm communicator) {
   104	    const size_t elements = matrix.size();
   105	    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
   106	        const size_t remaining = elements - offset;
   107	        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, kMpiChunkElements));
   108	        MPI_Bcast(matrix.data() + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	void gatherMatrix(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& globalC,
   113	                  const size_t N, const size_t firstRow,
   114	                  const int rank, const int worldSize, MPI_Comm communicator) {
   115	    const size_t globalElements = N * N;
   116	    if (rank == 0) {
   117	        globalC.resize(globalElements);
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    // Gatherv is the fast path and covers all practical benchmark sizes.  Its
   121	    // counts and displacements are int-sized, so use ordered chunked sends as
   122	    // a fallback for very large allocations.
   123	    const bool canUseGatherv = globalElements <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
   124	    if (canUseGatherv) {
   125	        std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   126	        std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   127	        const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   128	        const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   129	
   130	        for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
   131	            const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   132	            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
   133	                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), remainder);
   134	            receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);
   135	            displacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processFirstRow * N);
   136	        }
   137	
   138	        MPI_Gatherv(localC.empty() ? nullptr : localC.data(),
   139	                    static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
   140	                    rank == 0 ? globalC.data() : nullptr, receiveCounts.data(),
   141	                    displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
   142	        return;
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    if (rank == 0) {
   146	        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(), globalC.begin() + firstRow * N);
   147	        for (int process = 1; process < worldSize; ++process) {
   148	            const size_t rows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) +
   149	                                (static_cast<size_t>(process) < N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) ? 1 : 0);
   150	            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * (N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize)) +
   151	                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   152	            const size_t elements = rows * N;
   153	            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
   154	                const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(elements - offset, kMpiChunkElements));
   155	                MPI_Recv(globalC.data() + processFirstRow * N + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
   156	                         process, 0, communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   157	            }
   158	        }
   159	    } else {
   160	        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localC.size(); offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
   161	            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(localC.size() - offset, kMpiChunkElements));
   162	            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 0, communicator);
   163	        }
   164	    }
   165	}
   166	
   167	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   168	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   169	
   170	    int rank = 0;
   171	    int worldSize = 1;
   172	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   173	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   174	
   175	    size_t N = 512;
   176	    bool validate = false;
   177	    bool printResults = false;
   178	    int parseResult = 0;
   179	
   180	    // Parse once and broadcast the configuration so all ranks follow exactly
   181	    // the same collective communication path.
   182	    unsigned long long nValue = static_cast<unsigned long long>(N);
   183	    int validateValue = 0;
   184	    int printResultsValue = 0;
   185	    if (rank == 0) {
   186	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   187	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   188	                const long parsedN = std::strtol(argv[++i], nullptr, 10);
   189	                if (parsedN <= 0) {
   190	                    printf("Matrix size must be a positive integer.\n");
   191	                    parseResult = 1;
   192	                    break;
   193	                }
   194	                N = static_cast<size_t>(parsedN);
   195	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   196	                validate = true;
   197	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   198	                printResults = true;
   199	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   200	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   201	                parseResult = 2;
   202	                break;
   203	            } else {
   204	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   205	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   206	                parseResult = 1;
   207	                break;
   208	            }
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        if (parseResult == 0) {
   212	            if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N) {
   213	                printf("Matrix size is too large.\n");
   214	                parseResult = 1;
   215	            } else {
   216	                nValue = static_cast<unsigned long long>(N);
   217	                validateValue = validate ? 1 : 0;
   218	                printResultsValue = printResults ? 1 : 0;
   219	            }
   220	        }
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    MPI_Bcast(&parseResult, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   224	    if (parseResult != 0) {
   225	        MPI_Finalize();
   226	        return parseResult == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    MPI_Bcast(&nValue, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   230	    MPI_Bcast(&validateValue, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   231	    MPI_Bcast(&printResultsValue, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   232	    N = static_cast<size_t>(nValue);
   233	    validate = validateValue != 0;
   234	    printResults = printResultsValue != 0;
   235	
   236	    const size_t worldSizeAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   237	    const size_t baseRows = N / worldSizeAsSize;
   238	    const size_t remainder = N % worldSizeAsSize;
   239	    const size_t localRows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   240	    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
   241	                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
   242	    const size_t matrixElements = N * N;
   243	
   244	    if (rank == 0) {
   245	        printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   246	        printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
   247	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   248	        printf("MPI processes: %d\n", worldSize);
   249	        printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    // A is generated locally from its global row number.  Only B is
   253	    // replicated, which avoids broadcasting or scattering the input A matrix.
   254	    std::vector<double> localA(localRows * N);
   255	    std::vector<double> B(matrixElements);
   256	    std::vector<double> localC(localRows * N);
   257	    initMatrixRows(localA, localRows, N, firstRow);
   258	    if (rank == 0) {
   259	        initMatrix(B, N);
   260	    }
   261	    broadcastMatrix(B, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   262	
   263	    // Retain full A only on rank zero when validation is requested, matching
   264	    // the original validation algorithm without imposing that memory cost on
   265	    // normal distributed benchmark runs.
   266	    std::vector<double> A;
   267	    std::vector<double> C;
   268	    if (rank == 0 && validate) {
   269	        A.resize(matrixElements);
   270	        initMatrix(A, N);
   271	    }
   272	    if (rank == 0 && (validate || printResults)) {
   273	        C.resize(matrixElements);
   274	    }
   275	
   276	    if (rank == 0) {
   277	        printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   278	    }
   279	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   280	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   281	    matrixMultiply(localA, B, localC, localRows, N);
   282	    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   283	
   284	    double durationSeconds = 0.0;
   285	    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &durationSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   286	
   287	    if (rank == 0) {
   288	        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(durationSeconds * 1000.0);
   289	        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
   290	
   291	        const double gflops = durationSeconds > 0.0
   292	                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
   293	                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
   294	                                        durationSeconds / 1e9
   295	                                  : 0.0;
   296	        printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   297	    }
   298	
   299	    const bool needGlobalResult = validate || printResults;
   300	    if (needGlobalResult) {
   301	        gatherMatrix(localC, C, N, firstRow, rank, worldSize, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   302	    }
   303	
   304	    int exitCode = 0;
   305	    if (rank == 0) {
   306	        // Print results for external validation
   307	        if (printResults) {
   308	            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
   309	        }
   310	
   311	        // Validation
   312	        if (validate) {
   313	            printf("Validating result...\n");
   314	            const bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
   315	
   316	            if (valid) {
   317	                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   318	            } else {
   319	                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   320	                exitCode = 1;
   321	            }
   322	        }
   323	    }
   324	
   325	    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   326	    MPI_Finalize();
   327	    return exitCode;
   328	}

+// MPI point-to-point calls use an int element count on all commonly deployed
+// MPI versions.  Chunking keeps the code correct for matrices whose flat
+// storage exceeds that limit, while normal benchmark-sized matrices use one
+// collective call.
+constexpr size_t kMpiChunkElements = 1U << 20;
+
+void broadcastMatrix(std::vector<double>& matrix, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t elements = matrix.size();
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+        const size_t remaining = elements - offset;
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, kMpiChunkElements));
+        MPI_Bcast(matrix.data() + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
+    }
+}

+void gatherMatrix(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& globalC,
+                  const size_t N, const size_t firstRow,
+                  const int rank, const int worldSize, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t globalElements = N * N;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalC.resize(globalElements);
+    }
+
+    // Gatherv is the fast path and covers all practical benchmark sizes.  Its
+    // counts and displacements are int-sized, so use ordered chunked sends as
+    // a fallback for very large allocations.
+    const bool canUseGatherv = globalElements <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (canUseGatherv) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+        std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+        const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+        const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+
+        for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+            const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
+                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), remainder);
+            receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);
+            displacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processFirstRow * N);
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.empty() ? nullptr : localC.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? globalC.data() : nullptr, receiveCounts.data(),
+                    displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(), globalC.begin() + firstRow * N);
+        for (int process = 1; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+            const size_t rows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) +
+                                (static_cast<size_t>(process) < N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) ? 1 : 0);
+            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * (N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize)) +
+                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            const size_t elements = rows * N;
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+                const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(elements - offset, kMpiChunkElements));
+                MPI_Recv(globalC.data() + processFirstRow * N + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE,

+            }
+        }

+
+// Multiply local A rows by the complete B matrix.  The k order is identical
+// to the original i-j-k kernel, so the floating-point result is unchanged.
+// With k as the middle loop, B and each C row are accessed contiguously and
+// the innermost loop can be vectorized by the compiler.
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        const double* const aRow = A.data() + i * N;
+        double* const cRow = C.data() + i * N;
+        std::fill(cRow, cRow + N, 0.0);
+
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            const double aValue = aRow[k];
+            const double* const bRow = B.data() + k * N;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+                cRow[j] += aValue * bRow[j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+// MPI point-to-point calls use an int element count on all commonly deployed
+// MPI versions.  Chunking keeps the code correct for matrices whose flat
+// storage exceeds that limit, while normal benchmark-sized matrices use one
+// collective call.
+constexpr size_t kMpiChunkElements = 1U << 20;
+
+void broadcastMatrix(std::vector<double>& matrix, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t elements = matrix.size();
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+        const size_t remaining = elements - offset;
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, kMpiChunkElements));
+        MPI_Bcast(matrix.data() + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+void gatherMatrix(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& globalC,
+                  const size_t N, const size_t firstRow,
+                  const int rank, const int worldSize, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t globalElements = N * N;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalC.resize(globalElements);
+    }
+
+    // Gatherv is the fast path and covers all practical benchmark sizes.  Its
+    // counts and displacements are int-sized, so use ordered chunked sends as
+    // a fallback for very large allocations.
+    const bool canUseGatherv = globalElements <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (canUseGatherv) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+        std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+        const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+        const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+
+        for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+            const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
+                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), remainder);
+            receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);
+            displacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processFirstRow * N);
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.empty() ? nullptr : localC.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? globalC.data() : nullptr, receiveCounts.data(),
+                    displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(), globalC.begin() + firstRow * N);
+        for (int process = 1; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+            const size_t rows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) +
+                                (static_cast<size_t>(process) < N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) ? 1 : 0);
+            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * (N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize)) +
+                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            const size_t elements = rows * N;
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+                const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(elements - offset, kMpiChunkElements));
+                MPI_Recv(globalC.data() + processFirstRow * N + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         process, 0, communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localC.size(); offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(localC.size() - offset, kMpiChunkElements));
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 0, communicator);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
+        }
+
+        // Validation
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);

+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }

+
+// Multiply local A rows by the complete B matrix.  The k order is identical
+// to the original i-j-k kernel, so the floating-point result is unchanged.
+// With k as the middle loop, B and each C row are accessed contiguously and
+// the innermost loop can be vectorized by the compiler.
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        const double* const aRow = A.data() + i * N;
+        double* const cRow = C.data() + i * N;
+        std::fill(cRow, cRow + N, 0.0);
+
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            const double aValue = aRow[k];
+            const double* const bRow = B.data() + k * N;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+                cRow[j] += aValue * bRow[j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+// MPI point-to-point calls use an int element count on all commonly deployed
+// MPI versions.  Chunking keeps the code correct for matrices whose flat
+// storage exceeds that limit, while normal benchmark-sized matrices use one
+// collective call.
+constexpr size_t kMpiChunkElements = 1U << 20;
+
+void broadcastMatrix(std::vector<double>& matrix, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t elements = matrix.size();
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+        const size_t remaining = elements - offset;
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, kMpiChunkElements));
+        MPI_Bcast(matrix.data() + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+void gatherMatrix(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& globalC,
+                  const size_t N, const size_t firstRow,
+                  const int rank, const int worldSize, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t globalElements = N * N;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalC.resize(globalElements);
+    }
+
+    // Gatherv is the fast path and covers all practical benchmark sizes.  Its
+    // counts and displacements are int-sized, so use ordered chunked sends as
+    // a fallback for very large allocations.
+    const bool canUseGatherv = globalElements <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (canUseGatherv) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+        std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+        const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+        const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+
+        for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+            const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
+                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), remainder);
+            receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);
+            displacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processFirstRow * N);
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.empty() ? nullptr : localC.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? globalC.data() : nullptr, receiveCounts.data(),
+                    displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(), globalC.begin() + firstRow * N);
+        for (int process = 1; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+            const size_t rows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) +
+                                (static_cast<size_t>(process) < N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) ? 1 : 0);
+            const size_t processFirstRow = static_cast<size_t>(process) * (N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize)) +
+                                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            const size_t elements = rows * N;
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < elements; offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+                const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(elements - offset, kMpiChunkElements));
+                MPI_Recv(globalC.data() + processFirstRow * N + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         process, 0, communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localC.size(); offset += kMpiChunkElements) {
+            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(localC.size() - offset, kMpiChunkElements));
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, count, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 0, communicator);
+        }
+    }
+}
